= \"essential\"\ndifficulty = \"hard\"\ndifficulty_explanation = \"\"\"\nThe report gives only a symptom and a screen recording: in a collaborative session the text\nreplicates fine, but caret and selection positions end up wrong. Nothing throws, nothing logs,\nand four independent defects across three files contribute to it.\n\n1. `useYjsCollaboration` (packages/lexical-react/src/shared/useYjsCollaboration.tsx), the hook\n   behind the main (non-experimental) `CollaborationPlugin`, never subscribes to the Yjs\n   Awareness 'update' event, so cursor rendering re-runs only from the Yjs document\n   tree-change listener -- which is why the reporter observed that the position after typing\n   is still synced. Consequence: a collaborator's caret on a blank line does not appear, a\n   caret at the end of a paragraph is drawn in the NEXT paragraph, and a selection ending on a\n   blank line has that end land in the wrong place.\n\nDefects 1-3 are restoration-shaped: each has a working analogue within a screen or two (the v2\nhook, the creation branch three lines up, the same helper call in the sibling branch), and a\nprior trial repaired all three in about four minutes. Defect 4 is deliberately not of that\nshape. The correct algorithm is REPLACED by a plausible wrong one rather than deleted, so\nthere is no adjacent line to copy; the v1 content-unit layout (a text child occupies 1 + length\nunits, any other child 1) has to be derived from the decode walk in Utils.ts and from the node\nsizes, and the v2 encoder 30 lines below is a trap -- it counts children against the node's own\nshared type, which is right for v2's layout and wrong for v1's. Three independently observable defects in one visible feature -- where\\ncarets and selections end up during collaborative editing -- across three files and both\\ndirections of the collaboration pipeline:\\n\\n  B1 (upstream, already present at base_commit 34cab58e; the subject of issue #7959 / PR\\n      #7960): `useYjsCollaboration` -- the hook behind the main, non-experimental\\n      `CollaborationPlugin` -- never subscribes to the Yjs Awareness 'update' event, so a\\n      purely ephemeral broadcast never reaches `syncCursorPositions`. Cursor rendering is\\n      re-run only from the Yjs document tree-change listener, which is why the reporter\\n      observed that \\\"the position of the cursor after typing is still synced\\\".\\n\\n  B2 (injected, environment/bugs/01-remote-cursor-never-repositioned.patch):\\n      `syncCursorPositions` (packages/lexical-yjs/src/SyncCursors.ts) creates a remote\\n      cursor's selection the first time it sees a participant but never rewrites it, so a\\n      remote caret freezes at the first place it appeared.\\n\\n  B3 (injected, 02-local-selection-not-reanchored.patch):\\n      `$syncCursorFromYjs` (packages/lexical-yjs/src/SyncEditorStates.ts) no longer\\n      re-derives the LOCAL editor's own selection from the relative position that client\\n      published when a remote change is applied -- the non-recovery branch that did so is\\n      deleted. Consequence: your own caret keeps a raw offset and drifts through the text\\n      whenever somebody edits earlier content, so your next keystroke lands in the wrong\\n      place.\\n\\n  B4 (injected, 03-structural-point-mapping.patch): `createRelativePosition`\\n      (packages/lexical-yjs/src/SyncCursors.ts) encodes a STRUCTURAL (element) point -- the\\n      kind a caret has on a blank line or at a paragraph boundary -- against the PARENT's\\n      shared type at the element's own slot, the way a text point is encoded, instead of\\n      against the element's own shared type at the accumulated content offset of the child\\n      boundary. Consequence: a collaborator's caret on a blank line does not appear, a caret\\n      at the end of a paragraph is drawn in the NEXT paragraph, and a selection ending on a\\n      blank line has that end land somewhere the paragraph cannot hold.\\n\\nWHY B4 IS NOT ANOTHER RESTORATION. The first three defects are all \\\"a branch or call was\\ndeleted and the answer is visible within a screen\\\": B1's analogue is the v2 hook 130 lines\\nbelow, B2's is the creation branch three lines above, B3's is the identical helper call 15\\nlines above in the sibling branch -- and an official trial repaired all three in 4m09s. B4\\nREPLACES the algorithm with a plausible wrong one instead of deleting it, so there is no\\nadjacent line to copy back. The correct encoding has to be derived from v1's content-unit\\nlayout (a text child occupies 1 + length units in its parent's XmlText, any other child 1),\\nwhich is stated nowhere in the encoder and has to be read out of the decode walk\\n(getPositionFromElementAndOffset in Utils.ts, plus the getSize() implementations). The nearest\\nanalogue -- createRelativePositionV2, 30 lines below -- is a TRAP rather than a twin: it counts\\nchildren against the node's own shared type, which is correct for v2's XmlElement layout and\\nwrong for v1's, and a candidate who copies it still fails the paragraph-boundary test.\\nMeasured: the latest official trial's patch, replayed byte-for-byte, scores 0.0 with 11/14.\\n\\nWHY B3 IS THE RIGHT THIRD DEFECT. It is the same feature (collaborative caret/selection\\nposition) but the opposite direction: B1 and B2 are about how OTHER participants' cursors are\\ndelivered and drawn, B3 is about the local client's own selection surviving a remote DOCUMENT\\nchange. It is a pure deletion of known-good upstream code, needs no invented API, lives in a\\nthird file with no nearby analogue to copy from, and repairing it requires understanding that\\na collaborative caret is anchored to a CRDT position rather than to a numeric offset.\\nMeasured: the completed official trial (which fixed exactly B1 and B2 and scored 1.0 against\\nthe previous two-defect baseline) now scores 0.0 with 8/11.\\n\\nINJECTION MECHANICS. All three injected patches are good -> buggy patches of upstream code, inlined\\nin environment/Dockerfile as base64 with their full text reproduced verbatim in the comment\\nimmediately above each one. They are decoded, applied and deleted inside the SAME RUN layer,\\nbefore .git is stripped and before the harbor_base commit is created -- so they exist in no\\nimage layer's filesystem, are never copied into /app/.harbor_assets/, and the agent cannot\\nread them. They are inlined rather than shipped as sidecar files because the canonical bundle\\nlayout admits only environment/Dockerfile and environment/assets/*: a patch under\\nenvironment/assets/bugs/ fails the shipping integrity gate on two checks\\n(asset-documented-both-files, asset-referenced-in-instruction, verified empirically, 16\\npassed / 2 failed), and a sidecar anywhere else under environment/ is reported as a stray file\\nwhose offered repair is to delete it -- which would break the build with a missing COPY\\nsource. Inlining is the project's own documented resolution for Dockerfile-auxiliary content\\nand leaves the bundle at 18/18 integrity with no stray-file finding for the patches. Verified in the built image: one commit (\\\"base\\\") plus\\nthe harbor_base tag, `git diff harbor_base` empty, no patch file anywhere, no /tmp remnant,\\ntests/ and solution/ absent. Every injection is a pure deletion, so the tree carries no added\\nline, comment or marker that could hint at a repair.\\n\\nA FOCUS/BLUR DEFECT WAS CONSIDERED AND REMAINS REJECTED: the supplied recording never shows a\\ncollaborator caret disappearing (confirmed across 140 frames sampled at 10fps over the whole\\n14s clip), so nothing in the visual evidence establishes that a caret is meant to vanish when\\na participant stops working. It was not re-added.\\n\\nTEST SUITE. One self-contained file,\\npackages/lexical-react/src/__tests__/unit/CollaborationAwareness.test.tsx (test.patch adds\\nonly this file). No assertion names a source path, symbol, event, hook or branch.\\n\\njsdom capability shims, applied identically to every candidate and documented in the file:\\nHTMLElement.offsetParent, Range.prototype.getClientRects, Range.prototype.getBoundingClientRect\\nand Element.prototype.scrollIntoView. The last two were added for the local-selection tests:\\ncommitting a selection synchronously drives Lexical's DOM-selection reconciliation, which in\\njsdom otherwise throws on a missing Range.getBoundingClientRect. Nothing asserts on pixel\\ngeometry and there are no sleeps beyond a single macrotask flush that lets queued reactions to\\nan edit run before anything is asserted.\\n\\nThe local-selection tests give the observing participant real document focus. That is\\nload-bearing rather than cosmetic: without it jsdom has no DOM selection to reconcile against\\nand a follow-up editor update re-derives the selection from an empty DOM selection, collapsing\\nit to offset 0 on a correct build as well as a broken one. With real focus the correct build\\nre-anchors to the expected offset and the broken build leaves the caret at its stale offset --\\nverified by tracing both. Test 10 deletes few enough characters that the local offset\\nstays valid, so the pre-existing recovery path cannot mask the defect. Test 11 uses a backward\\nselection, so a repair that silently normalises direction is caught. on(type: string, cb: (data: never) => void): void {\n252\t+    let set = this.listeners.get(type);\n253\t+    if (set === undefined) {\n254\t+      set = new Set();\n255\t+      this.listeners.set(type, set);\n256\t+    }\n257\t+    set.add(cb);\n258\t+  }\n259\t+\n260\t+  off(type: string, cb: (data: never) => void): void {\n261\t+    const set = this.doc.toJSON();\n269\t+  }\n270\t+\n271\t+  private send(update: Uint8Array): void {\n272\t+    for (const client of this.connection.clients) {\n273\t+      if (client === this) {\n274\t+        continue;\n275\t+      }\n276\t+      if (client.connected) {\n277\t+        Y.applyUpdate(client.doc, update, this.connection);\n278\t+      } else {\n279\t+        client.buffered.push(update);\n280\t+      }\n281\t+    }\n282\t+  }\n283\t+\n284\t+  private dispatch(type: string, data: unknown): void {\n285\t+    const set = this.listeners.get(type);\n286\t+    if (set !== undefined) {\n287\t+      set.forEach((cb) => (cb as (arg: unknown) => void)(data));\n288\t+    }\n289\t+  }\n290\t+}\n291\t+\n292\t+async function waitForReact(cb: () => void): });\n304\t+}\n305\t+\n306\t+/**\n307\t+ * One participant's editor, assembled from the same public collaboration hooks\n308\t+ * the shipped collaboration plugin composes. );\n335\t+\n336\t+  useEffect(() => {\n337\t+    onReady(editor, binding);\n338\t+    // onReady is a fresh closure each render by design (it captures per-test\n339\t+    // state); only re-fire when );\n354\t+  useYjsFocusTracking(editor, provider, name, color);\n355\t+\n356\t+  return (\n357\t+    <>\n358\t+      <ContentEditable />\n359\t+      {cursors}\n360\t+    </>\n361\t+  );\n362\t+}\n363\t+\n364\t+/**\n365\t+ * How one participant currently sees another. `visible: false` means the other\n366\t+ * participant is known to be in the session but has nothing to show right */\n552\t+  async place(anchorOffset: number, focusOffset: number): Promise<void> {\n553\t+    await waitForReact(() => {\n554\t+      this.editor!.update(\n555\t+        () => {\n556\t+ paragraphs.length; i++) {\n578\t+        const paragraph = paragraphs[i];\n579\t+        if (paragraph.getKey() === key) {\n580\t+          if (!$isElementNode(paragraph)) {\n581\t+            return {paragraph: i, place: 'invalid'} as Place;\n582\t+          }\n583\t+          const children = paragraph.getChildren();\n584\t+          if (offset > children.length) {\n585\t+            return {paragraph: i, place: 'invalid'} as Place;\n586\t+          }\n587\t+          let characters = 0;\n588\t+          for (let c = 0; c < offset; c++) {\n589\t+            characters += children[c].getTextContentSize();\n590\t+          }\n591\t+          return {characters, paragraph: i} as Place;\n592\t+        }\n593\t+        if ($isElementNode(paragraph)) {\n594\t+ }\n613\t+\n614\t+  /** Where this participant can see the two ends of `other`'s selection. */\n615\t+  seenEnds(other: Peer): {anchor: Place; focus: Place} | 'not shown' {\n616\t+    const cursor = LexicalEditor,\n+  ParagraphNode,\n+  TextNode,\n+} from 'lexical';\n+import * as React from 'react';\n+import {act, useEffect, useMemo, useState} from 'react';\n+import {createRoot, type Root} from '../../shared/useYjsCollaboration';\n+\n+/**\n+ * ===========================================================================\n+ * What this file tests\n+ * ===========================================================================\n+ * Two or more people editing the same document at the same time. Besides the\n+ * text itself, each participant sees where the others are: their caret and the\n+ * range they have selected. This file drives that end to end -- real editors,\n+ * real document replication, a real cross-client presence channel -- and asserts\n+ * only on what one participant can observe about the others.\n+ *\n+ * Everything here is deliberately observational. No assertion names a source\n+ * file, a function, an event, a hook or a subscription: any implementation that\n+ * puts the right participant state on screen at the right time passes, and any\n+ * implementation that does not, does not.\n+ *\n+ * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+ * jsdom capability shims\n+ * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+ * The two shims below exist only because jsdom ships no CSS layout engine. They\n+ * are unrelated to the behaviour under test and apply identically to every\n+ * candidate:\n+ *\n+ *  1. `HTMLElement.offsetParent` is hardcoded to `null` for every element in\n+ *     jsdom, where a real browser returns the nearest positioned ancestor.\n+ *     Collaboration cursor rendering reads a `null` offsetParent as \"this editor\n+ *     is not on screen\", so without this shim NO participant state would be\n+ *     resolved in this environment -- on a broken and on a correct build alike.\n+ *  2. `Range.prototype.getClientRects` / `getBoundingClientRect` do not exist in\n+ *     jsdom at all, and `Element.prototype.scrollIntoView` is missing. They are\n+ *     stubbed to report no rectangles / an empty rect / nothing, which is the\n+ *     honest answer where there is no layout. The tests therefore assert on\n+ *     resolved positions (document offsets and node identity) and never on pixel\n+ *     geometry, so nothing below depends on invented coordinates.\n+ */\n+Object.defineProperty(HTMLElement.prototype, 'offsetParent', {\n+  configurable: true,\n+  get(this: HTMLElement): Element | null {\n+    const {body} = this.ownerDocument;\n+    return this === body ? null : body;\n+  },\n+});\n+if (\n+  typeof (Range.prototype as {getClientRects?: unknown}).getClientRects !==\n+  'function'\n+) {\n+  (\n+    Range.prototype as unknown as {getClientRects: () => DOMRect[]}\n+  ).getClientRects = () => [];\n+}\n+if (\n+  typeof (Range.prototype as {getBoundingClientRect?: unknown})\n+    .getBoundingClientRect !== 'function'\n+) {\n+  (\n+    Range.prototype as unknown as {getBoundingClientRect: () => DOMRect}\n+  ).getBoundingClientRect = () => new DOMRect(0, 0, 0, 0);\n+}\n+if (typeof Element.prototype.scrollIntoView !== 'function') {\n+  Element.prototype.scrollIntoView = () => undefined;\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * The two collaboration types this file needs, derived from the public factory\n+ * rather than imported by name, so the tests do not depend on which type names a\n+ * candidate's implementation happens to export.\n+ */\n+type Binding = ReturnType<typeof createBinding>;\n+type ProviderLike = Parameters<typeof createBinding>[1];\n+\n+/** Whatever the collaboration layer chooses to publish about a participant. */\n+type EphemeralState = Record<string, unknown>;\n+\n+/**\n+ * A working cross-client presence channel.\n+ *\n+ * The presence half of a collaboration session is not part of the replicated\n+ * document: it is short-lived per-participant state (who is here and where their\n+ * caret is) that every client publishes and every other client is notified\n+ * about. This is a faithful, minimal implementation of\n+ * exactly that and nothing more: publishing local state synchronously notifies\n+ * every OTHER linked client's subscribers, with no payload, so subscribers\n+ * always re-read the full participant map -- as the real protocol behaves.\n+ *\n+ * The suite brings its own rather than reusing the one in `./utils.tsx`, whose\n+ * presence object is a deliberate stub (subscribe/unsubscribe are no-ops and it\n+ * reports a single hardcoded participant id that the cursor layer treats as \"not\n+ * a real participant\"). That stub is fine for tests that only care about\n+ * document text, and it is intentionally NOT edited here: a candidate must not be\n+ * able to change the harness by editing shared test infrastructure, so this file\n+ * is self-contained apart from the product code under test.\n+ */\n+class FakeAwareness {\n+  peers: FakeAwareness[] = [];\n+  private localState: EphemeralState | null = null;\n+  private listeners = new Set<() => void>();\n+\n+  constructor(readonly id: number) {}\n+\n+  getLocalState = (): EphemeralState | null => this.localState;\n+\n+  getStates = (): Map<number, EphemeralState> => {\n+    const states = new Map<number, EphemeralState>();\n+    for (const peer of this.peers) {\n+      if (peer !== this && peer.localState !== null) {\n+        states.set(peer.id, peer.localState);\n+      }\n+    }\n+    return states;\n+  };\n+\n+  setLocalState = (state: EphemeralState): void => {\n+    this.localState = state;\n+    this.broadcast();\n+  };\n+\n+  setLocalStateField = (field: string, value: unknown): void => {\n+    this.setLocalState({\n+      ...(this.localState as EphemeralState),\n+      [field]: value,\n+    });\n+  };\n+\n+  /**\n+   * The participant's presence disappears entirely -- they closed the tab, or\n+   * the session dropped them -- with no document mutation of any kind.\n+   */\n+  clearLocalState(): void {\n+    this.localState = null;\n+    this.broadcast();\n+  }\n+\n+  on = (type: string, cb: () => void): void => {\n+    // Checked at run time, not only through a TypeScript parameter type, so a\n+    // candidate that subscribes to some other event name genuinely receives no\n+    // callbacks -- exactly as it would against the real protocol.\n+    if (type === 'update') {\n+      this.listeners.add(cb);\n+    }\n+  };\n+\n+  off = (type: string, cb: () => void): void => {\n+    if (type === 'update') {\n+      this.listeners.delete(cb);\n+    }\n+  };\n+\n+  private broadcast(): void {\n+    for (const peer of this.peers) {\n+      if (peer !== this) {\n+        // Snapshot before iterating: a subscriber reacting to this publication\n+        // may add or remove another subscriber as a side effect.\n+        Array.from(peer.listeners).forEach((cb) => cb());\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * The transport: a set of clients replicating one document to each other, each\n+ * with its own replica, buffering updates for clients that are offline. Same\n+ * shape as the connection the other collaboration tests use, inlined so this\n+ * file's harness cannot be altered from outside the file.\n+ */\n+class TestConnection {\n+  clients: TestClient[] = [];\n+\n+  createClient(): TestClient {\n+    const client = new TestClient(this);\n+    this.clients.push(client);\n+    return client;\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+class TestClient {\n+  readonly doc: Y.Doc = new Y.Doc({gc: false});\n+  readonly awareness: FakeAwareness;\n+  connected = false;\n+  private buffered: Uint8Array[] = [];\n+  private listeners = new Map<string, Set<(data: never) => void>>();\n+\n+  constructor(private connection: TestConnection) {\n+    // A real presence protocol identifies a participant by the same id their\n+    // document replica uses, so this does too.\n+    this.awareness = new FakeAwareness(this.doc.clientID);\n+    this.doc.on('update', (update: Uint8Array, origin: unknown) => {\n+      if (origin !== this.connection && this.connected) {\n+        this.send(update);\n+      }\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  connect(): void {\n+    if (this.connected) {\n+      return;\n+    }\n+    this.connected = true;\n+    const state = Y.encodeStateAsUpdate(this.doc);\n+    if (this.buffered.length > 0) {\n+      Y.applyUpdate(this.doc, Y.mergeUpdates(this.buffered), this.connection);\n+      this.buffered = [];\n+    }\n+    this.send(state);\n+    this.dispatch('sync', true);\n+  }\n+\n+  disconnect(): void {\n+    this.connected = false;\n+  }\n+\n+  on(type: string, cb: (data: never) => void): void {\n+    let set = this.listeners.get(type);\n+    if (set === undefined) {\n+      set = new Set();\n+      this.listeners.set(type, set);\n+    }\n+    set.add(cb);\n+  }\n+\n+  off(type: string, cb: (data: never) => void): void {\n+    const set = this.listeners.get(type);\n+    if (set !== undefined) {\n+      set.delete(cb);\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  documentJSON(): unknown {\n+    return this.doc.toJSON();\n+  }\n+\n+  private send(update: Uint8Array): void {\n+    for (const client of this.connection.clients) {\n+      if (client === this) {\n+        continue;\n+      }\n+      if (client.connected) {\n+        Y.applyUpdate(client.doc, update, this.connection);\n+      } else {\n+        client.buffered.push(update);\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  private dispatch(type: string, data: unknown): void {\n+    const set = this.listeners.get(type);\n+    if (set !== undefined) {\n+      set.forEach((cb) => (cb as (arg: unknown) => void)(data));\n+    }\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+async function waitForReact(cb: () => void): Promise<void> {\n+  await act(async () => {\n+    cb();\n+    await Promise.resolve().then();\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+/** Let every queued reaction to an edit run before anything is asserted. */\n+async function settle(): Promise<void> {\n+  await act(async () => {\n+    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));\n+  });\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * One participant's editor, assembled from the same public collaboration hooks\n+ * the shipped collaboration plugin composes. They are called directly rather\n+ * than through the plugin because the plugin never hands out the collaboration\n+ * binding it builds internally, and that binding is where the state a\n+ * participant can see about the others is resolved.\n+ */\n+function CollaborativeEditor({\n+  id,\n+  provider,\n+  doc,\n+  name,\n+  color,\n+  onReady,\n+}: {\n+  id: string;\n+  provider: ProviderLike;\n+  doc: Y.Doc;\n+  name: string;\n+  color: string;\n+  onReady: (editor: LexicalEditor, binding: Binding) => void;\n+}): React.JSX.Element {\n+  const [editor] = useLexicalComposerContext();\n+  const [, setDoc] = useState<Y.Doc | undefined>(doc);\n+  const docMap = useMemo(() => new Map([[id, doc]]), [id, doc]);\n+  const binding = useMemo(\n+    () => createBinding(editor, provider, id, doc, docMap),\n+    [editor, provider, id, doc, docMap],\n+  );\n+\n+  useEffect(() => {\n+    onReady(editor, binding);\n+    // onReady is a fresh closure each render by design (it captures per-test\n+    // state); only re-fire when the editor/binding identity actually changes.\n+    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n+  }, [editor, binding]);\n+\n+  const cursors = useYjsCollaboration(\n+    editor,\n+    id,\n+    provider,\n+    docMap,\n+    name,\n+    color,\n+    true,\n+    binding,\n+    setDoc,\n+  );\n+  useYjsFocusTracking(editor, provider, name, color);\n+\n+  return (\n+    <>\n+      <ContentEditable />\n+      {cursors}\n+    </>\n+  );\n+}\n+\n+/**\n+ * How one participant currently sees another. `visible: false` means the other\n+ * participant is known to be in the session but has nothing to show right now --\n+ * for instance because they are not working in their editor.\n+ */\n+/** A position in the document, independent of how the editor represents it. */\n+type Place =\n+  | {paragraph: number; characters: number}\n+  | {paragraph: number; place: 'invalid'}\n+  | {place: 'unknown'};\n+\n+type ParticipantView =\n+  | {inSession: false}\n+  | {inSession: true; label: string; visible: false}\n+  | {\n+      inSession: true;\n+      label: string;\n+      visible: true;\n+      anchorOffset: number;\n+      focusOffset: number;\n+      anchorOnParagraphText: boolean;\n+      focusOnParagraphText: boolean;\n+    };\n+\n+/** One simulated participant: a real editor plus a real replica and presence. */\n+class Peer {\n+  readonly client: TestClient;\n+  readonly name: string;\n+  readonly color: string;\n+  editor: LexicalEditor | null = null;\n+  binding: Binding | null = null;\n+  private container: HTMLDivElement;\n+  private root: Root;\n+\n+  constructor(client: TestClient, index: number) {\n+    this.client = client;\n+    this.name = `user-${index}`;\n+    this.color = `#00000${index}`;\n+    this.container = document.createElement('div');\n+    document.body.appendChild(this.container);\n+    this.root = createRoot(this.container);\n+  }\n+\n+  get awareness(): FakeAwareness {\n+    return this.client.awareness;\n+  }\n+\n+  async start(): Promise<void> {\n+    const provider = this.client as unknown as ProviderLike;\n+    await act(async () => {\n+      this.root.render(\n+        <LexicalComposer\n+          initialConfig={{\n+            editorState: null,\n+            namespace: this.name,\n+            onError: (e) => {\n+              throw e;\n+            },\n+          }}>\n+          <CollaborativeEditor\n+            id=\"main\"\n+            provider={provider}\n+            doc={this.client.doc}\n+            name={this.name}\n+            color={this.color}\n+            onReady={(editor, binding) => {\n+              this.editor = editor;\n+              this.binding = binding;\n+            }}\n+          />\n+        </LexicalComposer>,\n+      );\n+      await Promise.resolve().then();\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  stop(): void {\n+    act(() => {\n+      this.root.unmount();\n+    });\n+    this.container.parentNode?.removeChild(this.container);\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The text of the single shared paragraph this suite works with. */\n+  paragraphText(): string | null {\n+    return this.editor!.getEditorState().read(() => {\n+      const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>();\n+      const text = paragraph ? paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>() : null;\n+      return text ? text.getTextContent() : null;\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Identity of that paragraph's text node in THIS participant's editor. */\n+  paragraphTextKey(): string | null {\n+    return this.editor!.getEditorState().read(() => {\n+      const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>();\n+      const text = paragraph ? paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>() : null;\n+      return text ? text.getKey() : null;\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /** A fingerprint of the replicated document, for \"nothing was edited\" checks. */\n+  documentFingerprint(): string {\n+    return JSON.stringify(this.client.documentJSON());\n+  }\n+\n+  /** What this participant can currently see of `other`. */\n+  view(other: Peer): ParticipantView {\n+    const cursor = this.binding!.cursors.get(other.awareness.id);\n+    if (cursor === undefined) {\n+      return {inSession: false};\n+    }\n+    const selection = cursor.selection;\n+    if (selection === null) {\n+      return {inSession: true, label: cursor.name, visible: false};\n+    }\n+    const textKey = this.paragraphTextKey();\n+    return {\n+      anchorOffset: selection.anchor.offset,\n+      anchorOnParagraphText: selection.anchor.key === textKey,\n+      focusOffset: selection.focus.offset,\n+      focusOnParagraphText: selection.focus.key === textKey,\n+      inSession: true,\n+      label: cursor.name,\n+      visible: true,\n+    };\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Which other participants this participant is currently tracking. */\n+  trackedParticipants(): number[] {\n+    return Array.from(this.binding!.cursors.keys()).sort((a, b) => a - b);\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The participant puts their caret somewhere / selects a range. */\n+  async select(anchorOffset: number, focusOffset: number): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(() => {\n+        const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>()!;\n+        const text = paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>()!;\n+        text.select(anchorOffset, focusOffset);\n+      });\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The participant starts working in their editor. */\n+  async focusEditor(): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.getRootElement()!.dispatchEvent(new FocusEvent('focus'));\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /**\n+   * The participant's editor really is the one the browser is in -- their editor\n+   * holds the document focus, not just a focus notification. Only one editor per\n+   * document can be in this state, which is why the tests that need it give it to\n+   * the one participant whose own selection they observe.\n+   */\n+  async takeDocumentFocus(): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.getRootElement()!.focus();\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Where this participant's own caret / selection currently is. */\n+  ownSelection(): null | {\n+    anchorOffset: number;\n+    focusOffset: number;\n+    onParagraphText: boolean;\n+  } {\n+    const textKey = this.paragraphTextKey();\n+    return this.editor!.getEditorState().read(() => {\n+      const selection = $getSelection();\n+      if (!$isRangeSelection(selection)) {\n+        return null;\n+      }\n+      return {\n+        anchorOffset: selection.anchor.offset,\n+        focusOffset: selection.focus.offset,\n+        onParagraphText:\n+          selection.anchor.key === textKey && selection.focus.key === textKey,\n+      };\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /**\n+   * The participant puts their caret / selects a range, committed immediately --\n+   * the same thing a keystroke or a click does before the next event is handled.\n+   */\n+  async place(anchorOffset: number, focusOffset: number): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(\n+        () => {\n+          const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>()!;\n+          const text = paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>()!;\n+          text.select(anchorOffset, focusOffset);\n+        },\n+        {discrete: true},\n+      );\n+    });\n+    await settle();\n+  }\n+\n+  /**\n+   * Where a position sits in the document, said in a way that does not depend on\n+   * how the editor happens to represent it. A caret at the end of \"hello\" is the\n+   * same place whether it is held as \"5 characters into that text\" or as \"after\n+   * the first child of that paragraph\", so both are reported the same way here.\n+   * A position that its paragraph cannot actually hold is reported as `invalid`,\n+   * because that is what it is -- not a place in the document.\n+   */\n+  placeOf(key: string, offset: number): Place {\n+    return this.editor!.getEditorState().read(() => {\n+      const paragraphs = $getRoot().getChildren();\n+      for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {\n+        const paragraph = paragraphs[i];\n+        if (paragraph.getKey() === key) {\n+          if (!$isElementNode(paragraph)) {\n+            return {paragraph: i, place: 'invalid'} as Place;\n+          }\n+          const children = paragraph.getChildren();\n+          if (offset > children.length) {\n+            return {paragraph: i, place: 'invalid'} as Place;\n+          }\n+          let characters = 0;\n+          for (let c = 0; c < offset; c++) {\n+            characters += children[c].getTextContentSize();\n+          }\n+          return {characters, paragraph: i} as Place;\n+        }\n+        if ($isElementNode(paragraph)) {\n+          const children = paragraph.getChildren();\n+          for (let c = 0; c < children.length; c++) {\n+            const child = children[c];\n+            if (child.getKey() === key) {\n+              if (!$isTextNode(child) || offset > child.getTextContentSize()) {\n+                return {paragraph: i, place: 'invalid'} as Place;\n+              }\n+              let characters = offset;\n+              for (let b = 0; b < c; b++) {\n+                characters += children[b].getTextContentSize();\n+              }\n+              return {characters, paragraph: i} as Place;\n+            }\n+          }\n+        }\n+      }\n+      return {place: 'unknown'} as Place;\n+    });\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Where this participant can see the two ends of `other`'s selection. */\n+  seenEnds(other: Peer): {anchor: Place; focus: Place} | 'not shown' {\n+    const cursor = this.binding!.cursors.get(other.awareness.id);\n+    if (cursor === undefined || cursor.selection === null) {\n+      return 'not shown';\n+    }\n+    const {anchor, focus} = cursor.selection;\n+    return {\n+      anchor: this.placeOf(anchor.key, anchor.offset),\n+      focus: this.placeOf(focus.key, focus.offset),\n+    };\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Replace the document with one paragraph per entry ('' makes an empty one). */\n+  async writeParagraphs(texts: string[]): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(\n+        () => {\n+          const root = $getRoot();\n+          root.clear();\n+          for (const text of texts) {\n+            const paragraph = $createParagraphNode();\n+            if (text.length > 0) {\n+              paragraph.append($createTextNode(text));\n+            }\n+            root.append(paragraph);\n+          }\n+        },\n+        {discrete: true},\n+      );\n+    });\n+    await settle();\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The paragraph texts this participant currently sees. */\n+  paragraphTexts(): string[] {\n+    return this.editor!.getEditorState().read(() =>\n+      $getRoot()\n+        .getChildren()\n+        .map((paragraph) => paragraph.getTextContent()),\n+    );\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The participant puts their caret / selection wherever `place` says. */\n+  async placeSelection(place: () => void): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(place, {discrete: true});\n+    });\n+    await settle();\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The participant edits the paragraph text (any edit, anywhere in it). */\n+  async rewriteText(next: (current: string) => string): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(\n+        () => {\n+          const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>()!;\n+          const text = paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>()!;\n+          text.setTextContent(next(text.getTextContent()));\n+        },\n+        {discrete: true},\n+      );\n+    });\n+    await settle();\n+  }\n+\n+  /** The participant types at the end of the paragraph. */\n+  async appendText(suffix: string): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      this.editor!.update(() => {\n+        const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>()!;\n+        const text = paragraph.getFirstChild<TextNode>()!;\n+        text.setTextContent(text.getTextContent() + suffix);\n+      });\n+    });\n+  }\n+}\n+\n+describe('Collaboration: remote participant caret and selection state', () => {\n+  let peers: Peer[] = [];\n+\n+  afterEach(() => {\n+    for (const p of peers) {\n+      p.stop();\n+    }\n+    peers = [];\n+  });\n+\n+  async function makePeers(count: number): Promise<Peer[]> {\n+    const connection = new TestConnection();\n+    const made: Peer[] = [];\n+    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {\n+      made.push(new Peer(connection.createClient(), i));\n+    }\n+    const awarenesses = made.map((p) => p.awareness);\n+    for (const a of awarenesses) {\n+      a.peers = awarenesses;\n+    }\n+    for (const p of made) {\n+      await p.start();\n+    }\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      for (const p of made) {\n+        p.client.connect();\n+      }\n+    });\n+    peers = made;\n+    return made;\n+  }\n+\n+  /** Seed the shared paragraph from `peer`; everyone must receive it. */\n+  async function seedText(peer: Peer, text: string): Promise<void> {\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      peer.editor!.update(() => {\n+        const paragraph = $getRoot().getFirstChild<ParagraphNode>()!;\n+        paragraph.append($createTextNode(text));\n+      });\n+    });\n+    for (const p of peers) {\n+      expect(p.paragraphText()).toBe(text);\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  function visible(\n+    label: string,\n+    anchorOffset: number,\n+    focusOffset: number,\n+  ): ParticipantView {\n+    return {\n+      anchorOffset,\n+      anchorOnParagraphText: true,\n+      focusOffset,\n+      focusOnParagraphText: true,\n+      inSession: true,\n+      label,\n+      visible: true,\n+    };\n+  }\n+\n+  // ==========================================================================\n+  // A participant's caret and selected range must travel on their own. No\n+  // scenario below types a single character after the shared text is in place.\n+  // ==========================================================================\n+\n+  it('shows a remote caret that appeared from a selection-only change, with no document edit', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+\n+    const textBefore = local.paragraphText();\n+    const documentBefore = local.documentFingerprint();\n+\n+    // The only thing that happens: the other participant puts their caret\n+    // somewhere. Nothing is typed, deleted or otherwise written.\n+    await remote.select(4, 4);\n+\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 4, 4));\n+    // ... and it really was selection-only: the document did not move.\n+    expect(local.paragraphText()).toBe(textBefore);\n+    expect(local.documentFingerprint()).toBe(documentBefore);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('moves an already-visible remote caret to the position it moved to', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+\n+    await remote.select(2, 2);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 2, 2));\n+\n+    const documentBefore = local.documentFingerprint();\n+    await remote.select(9, 9);\n+\n+    // The caret is at the new position -- not merely still on screen.\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 9, 9));\n+    expect(local.documentFingerprint()).toBe(documentBefore);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('shows both ends of a remote selection range, in the direction it was made', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+\n+    await remote.select(2, 7);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 2, 7));\n+  });\n+\n+  it('replaces a remote selection range with the new range, with no document edit', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+\n+    await remote.select(2, 7);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 2, 7));\n+\n+    const documentBefore = local.documentFingerprint();\n+\n+    // A different range, this one selected right-to-left.\n+    await remote.select(9, 5);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 9, 5));\n+\n+    // ... and then collapsed back down to a plain caret.\n+    await remote.select(1, 1);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 1, 1));\n+\n+    expect(local.documentFingerprint()).toBe(documentBefore);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('follows several consecutive remote selection changes with no typing in between', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+\n+    const documentBefore = local.documentFingerprint();\n+    const steps: Array<[number, number]> = [\n+      [1, 1],\n+      [3, 6],\n+      [6, 3],\n+      [8, 8],\n+      [2, 10],\n+    ];\n+    for (const [anchorOffset, focusOffset] of steps) {\n+      await remote.select(anchorOffset, focusOffset);\n+      expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(\n+        visible(remote.name, anchorOffset, focusOffset),\n+      );\n+    }\n+    expect(local.documentFingerprint()).toBe(documentBefore);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('keeps two remote participant selections independent of each other', async () => {\n+    const [local, remoteB, remoteC] = await makePeers(3);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remoteB.focusEditor();\n+    await remoteC.focusEditor();\n+\n+    await remoteB.select(0, 2);\n+    await remoteC.select(4, 6);\n+    expect(local.view(remoteB)).toEqual(visible(remoteB.name, 0, 2));\n+    expect(local.view(remoteC)).toEqual(visible(remoteC.name, 4, 6));\n+\n+    // One of them moving must not disturb the other one's state.\n+    await remoteB.select(8, 10);\n+    expect(local.view(remoteB)).toEqual(visible(remoteB.name, 8, 10));\n+    expect(local.view(remoteC)).toEqual(visible(remoteC.name, 4, 6));\n+\n+    expect(local.trackedParticipants()).toEqual(\n+      [remoteB.awareness.id, remoteC.awareness.id].sort((a, b) => a - b),\n+    );\n+  });\n+\n+  it('stops showing a remote participant once they leave the session', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+    await remote.select(2, 5);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 2, 5));\n+\n+    await waitForReact(() => {\n+      remote.awareness.clearLocalState();\n+    });\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual({inSession: false});\n+    expect(local.trackedParticipants()).toEqual([]);\n+  });\n+\n+  it('keeps following a remote participant selection after that participant typed', async () => {\n+    const [local, remote] = await makePeers(2);\n+    await seedText(local, 'hello world');\n+    await remote.focusEditor();\n+    await remote.select(1, 1);\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 1, 1));\n+\n+    // Typing is the one thing that has never been in doubt: the text arrives and\n+    // the participant's caret is still on screen where they left it.\n+    await remote.appendText('!');\n+    expect(local.paragraphText()).toBe('hello world!');\n+    expect(local.view(remote)).toEqual(visible(remote.name, 1, 1));\n+\n+    // From here on they only move around. await local.place(15, 15);\n+\n+    // Somebody else removes \"brave \" from earlier in the paragraph.\n+    await remote.rewriteText((current) => current.replace('brave ', ''));\n+
